THIRD-PARTY NOTICES - DDC Data Viewer
======================================

DDC Data Viewer contains the open-source components listed below. Each is used under a permissive
licence (MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD, ISC, Zlib, Unicode or equivalent); where a component offers a choice,
the permissive option shown in brackets is used. Licence texts follow the list.
Microsoft Edge WebView2 Runtime is a Windows system component used at run time; it is not part of this
package (the installer only starts Microsoft's own installer when the runtime is missing).
